# Feature reference map — 9 October 2026

Purpose: unblock **foliage, light, ground and materials 2→3**, under the [ship bar and process cut](DECISIONS.md). This is a reference index, not a new audit queue, value approval, implementation claim or score. Read the feature row, then its exact-file catalog and STATUS before work. Web and native need their own evidence.

Authority and method: [INTEGRATION](INTEGRATION.md), [input INDEX](INDEX.md), [MOCKS](MOCKS.md), [proposal approval index](../proposals/INDEX.md), and [GRADING §§M/N](../lookloop/GRADING.md). Scanned tracked `docs/` and web evidence/report Markdown and JSON at `6e76ca7`; inventories, pack self-citations and this new map are excluded from usage. Exact paths/resolved links or unambiguous filenames count; explicit crown panel ranges are expanded against their manifest; pack-only mentions do not prove a specific sheet was used. Audit-only mentions are shown separately. Also scanned 69,965 removed report/grade lines in main history; removed citations link to their immutable parent revision. The only deleted Markdown path found was unrelated outreach. This establishes recorded citation history, not proof of unrecorded viewing or actual implementation. No code, captures, builds or new grades.

Camera classifications come from inspection of 324 existing source-board images/screens (temporary contact sheets, not new captures), their pack view descriptions and panel/gallery inheritance. `usable for aerial` means the stated inset/oblique/top view can inform that feature, **not** that it validates 600 m or Builder. Front/elevation charts and swatches are conservatively `street-level only`; they cannot establish an aerial crown volume. Presentation checks and original/superseded images are explicitly not new targets. Missing assets stay missing. Local pixels resolve from `~/Desktop/world-engine/` + repository path; tracked links do not promise images exist in a fresh clone.

## Camera and coverage gaps

| Needed target | What already exists | Actual gap and next comparison |
|---|---|---|
| 600 m aerial | Look-fix aerial continuation, water far rows, terrain/mountain and old visual-v2 aerials | No approved, height/FOV/season-matched **600 m** whole-scene target or per-aspect native/web acceptance pair. Do not treat 06-sloans street pixels as an aerial composition. Freeze an approved wide-view target and judge foliage/light/ground/materials with floor budget. |
| Builder top-down | Vegetation/ground top insets, event-sitemap import review and Creator Kit UI concepts | No approved complete engine-canvas target combining site core, coarse 3D ring, objects, shadows and selection at a declared top-down pose/scale. UI screenshots and oblique plans do not fill this gap. |
| Site-scoped stadium | Approved venues-campuses stadium-01…06: base/event street views + aerial planning; builder-event game-day | **Not “no stadium mock.”** Missing a selected real-site, data-bound top-down + oblique core/ring pair; generic stadium artwork supplies form, not site layout or a whole-city requirement. |
| Race-course site | Approved race-organizer Denver/Chicago/Greenville eye/aerial concepts; venues marathon/race village; mountain trail-race overlay | **Not “no race mock.”** Missing an end-to-end site-scoped course canvas with known extent, coarse ring and far readability; a race-village concept is not the entire course. |
| Tree/foliage far view | Crown distance-fade and far panels are front/elevation simplification; vegetation 06 includes aerial; landscape sheets show forest masses | No calibrated ≥400 m/600 m top/oblique crown-density target or matched transition sequence. “Far” on an isolated species card is not proof of far-scene coverage. Use A3 paired appearance + budget, not lossless 0/0 for intentional geometry changes. |
| AO, palette and facade roofs from above | Contact/crown top insets, house-archetype aerials | No isolated top-down acceptance targets separating contact shade, stable role albedo and roof simplification from lighting. Keep provisional traits explicit; do not fabricate a mock. |

## Exact-file catalog and usage

Inventory: **190 research/spec Markdown documents and 1,199 visual files** (including crops, galleries, source comparisons and phone checks; not 1,199 distinct approved mocks). The separate unused-file worklist excludes files with an exact current or historical report/spec/grade citation; audit-only and pack-only mentions do not count.

Each pack section lists every research/spec Markdown file and every visual entry for that pack in MOCKS. Shared references are listed once and linked from all relevant feature rows. JSON values/data remain available from each pack’s README/STATUS; inspect those keys before implementation. The file list includes derivatives so none silently becomes a new target. “No report/grade citation found” is a search result, not a consumption verdict; use INTEGRATION for code consumption. Audit-only citations do not satisfy a builder’s Used/Mock line. Historical use never approves a pending pack.

## Evidence and maintenance

Used: the approval STATUS files, feature research/specs, INTEGRATION/INDEX/MOCKS and retained report/grade citations linked above. Mock: the exact existing source-board images and inherited panels identified in this catalog. Deviation: concept distance labels are not measured camera altitude; absent exact-file citations remain unknown even where a pack is consumed. No image is promoted, edited or captured. Update exact usage and the affected INTEGRATION row in the implementation commit; A3 updates paired scores/scoreboard after merges.
